<div class="grid gap-primary lg:gap-x-[24px] lg:gap-y-[56px] grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 xl:grid-cols-6" :class="$store.shop.grid_big ? 'lg:grid-cols-3' : 'grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 xl:grid-cols-6'" x-transition="">
                        <template x-for="product in $store.shop.shop_list">
                            <div>
    <div x-data="{
        image_now: product.data_color[0]?.img_url ?? product.img_url,
        get color_filtered() {
            return $store.shop.color_selected
        },
        get is_sale_filter_active() {
            return $store.shop.filter?.condition?.includes('sale') ?? false;
        },
        get product_id() {
            return product.id;
        },
        init() {
            let only_flash_sale = false;
            if (only_flash_sale &amp;&amp; product.product_ids_flash_sale &amp;&amp; product.data_color) {
                let flashSaleIds = product.product_ids_flash_sale.split(',').map(Number);
                product.data_color = product.data_color.filter(c =&gt; flashSaleIds.includes(Number(c.id)));
            }

            this.$watch('color_filtered', () =&gt; this.handleFilterChange());
            this.$watch('is_sale_filter_active', () =&gt; this.handleFilterChange());
            this.$watch('product_id', () =&gt; this.handleFilterChange());
            this.handleFilterChange();
        },
        handleFilterChange() {
            let found = product.data_color?.find(c =&gt; this.color_filtered.includes(c.color));
            if (!found &amp;&amp; this.is_sale_filter_active) {
                const isOnSale = c =&gt; c.first_size &amp;&amp; c.first_size.normal_price &amp;&amp; c.first_size.normal_price &gt; c.first_size.price;
                found = product.data_color?.find(c =&gt; isOnSale(c) &amp;&amp; (c.calculate_stock ?? 0) &gt; 0)
                    ?? product.data_color?.find(isOnSale);
            }
            if (found) {
                this.selectColor(found);
            } else if (product.data_color &amp;&amp; product.data_color.length &gt; 0) {
                let defaultColor = product.data_color.find(c =&gt; c.id === product.id);
                
                if (!defaultColor || (defaultColor.calculate_stock ?? 0) &lt;= 0) {
                    let inStockColor = product.data_color.find(c =&gt; (c.calculate_stock ?? 0) &gt; 0);
                    if (inStockColor) {
                        defaultColor = inStockColor;
                    }
                }

                if (defaultColor) {
                    this.selectColor(defaultColor);
                } else {
                    this.selectColor(product.data_color[0]);
                }
            }
        },
        selectColor(color) {
            if (!color) return;
            this.selected_color = color;
            product.image = color.img_url;
            product.img_url = color.img_url;
            product.first_image = color.first_image;
            product.slug = color.slug;
            product.total_stock = color.calculate_stock ?? 0;
            product.thumbnail = color.img_url;
            this.image_now = color.img_url;
            
            // Clone first_size to avoid mutating original color.first_size
            product.first_size = color.first_size ? Object.assign({}, color.first_size) : null;
            product.price = color.price;

            // Flash Sale Calculation
            // Nilai flash sale diambil dari warna yang dipilih (color.flash_sale),
            // bukan dari field level grup — tiap warna bisa punya nominal/strike berbeda.
            let fs = color.flash_sale ?? null;
            product.flash_sale_id = fs ? fs.id : null;
            if (product.first_size &amp;&amp; fs) {
                let basePrice = Number(product.first_size.price ?? product.price ?? 0);
                let markup = Number(product.first_size.markup ?? 0);
                let currentTotal = basePrice + markup;
                let strikePrice = Number(fs.strike_price ?? 0);
                let flashSalePrice = 0;

                if (fs.type == 0) {
                    flashSalePrice = Number(fs.value_nominal ?? fs.value_percentage ?? 0);
                } else if (fs.type == 1) {
                    flashSalePrice = currentTotal * (Number(fs.value_percentage ?? 0) / 100);
                }

                if (flashSalePrice &gt; 0) {
                    let normalPrice = Number(product.first_size.normal_price ?? 0);
                    // Set normal price if it hasn't been set or if it's not a sale item yet
                    if (!normalPrice || normalPrice &lt;= basePrice) {
                        product.first_size.normal_price = strikePrice &gt; 0 ? strikePrice : basePrice;
                        product.first_size.markup_origin = strikePrice &gt; 0 ? 0 : markup;
                    } else if (strikePrice &gt; 0) {
                        product.first_size.normal_price = strikePrice;
                        product.first_size.markup_origin = 0;
                    }
                    let finalPrice = currentTotal - flashSalePrice;
                    product.first_size.price = finalPrice - markup;
                }
            }
        }
    }">
        <a :href="`https://bonsoir.id/product-detail/${product.slug}`">
            <img class="w-full object-cover max-lg:hidden" loading="lazy" :src="product?.image ?? product.img_url" :alt="product.designer_data?.name +' '+ product.name + (product.item_code ? ' - ' + product.item_code : '') + ' - ' + product.data_color[0]?.color" @mouseover="product.image = product.first_image?.image ?? product.img_url;" @mouseleave="product.image = product.thumbnail" :onerror="(event) =&gt; product.image = product?.thumbnail ?? product.img_url">

            <img class="w-full object-cover lg:hidden" loading="lazy" :src="product?.image ?? product.img_url" :alt="product.designer_data?.name +' '+ product.name + (product.item_code ? ' - ' + product.item_code : '') + ' - ' + product.data_color[0]?.color">
        </a>

        <div>
            <div class="py-2 flex gap-x-1 items-center ">
                <template x-if="product.data_color.length &gt; 0">
                    <template x-for="(color,index) in product.data_color">
                        <template x-if="index &lt; 5">
                            <div class="p-[2px] pb-[2px] cursor-pointer border hover:border-black-1000" :class="color.img_url === product.img_url ? 'border-black-1000' : 'border border-white'" @click="selectColor(color)">
                                <div class="lg:w-[14px] max-[370px]:w-[16px] w-[20px] h-[8px]" :style="{ background: color.color_code ? color.color_code : '#FFFFFF' }"></div>
                            </div>
                        </template>
                    </template>
                </template>

                <div class="text-xs text-gray-500" x-show="product.data_color.length &gt; 5">
                    + <span x-text="product.data_color.length - 5"></span>
                </div>
            </div>
            <a :href="`https://bonsoir.id/product-detail/${product.slug}`">
                <template x-if="product.total_stock &lt;= 0">
                    <label class="label label-danger !bg-gray-200 !text-gray-600 my-[8px] text-xs ">Sold
                        Out</label>
                </template>
                <template x-if="product.total_stock &gt; 0">
                    <div class="">
                        
                        <template x-if="product.is_defect">
                            <label class="label label-danger !bg-yellow-100 !text-yellow-700 my-[8px] text-xs">Small Defect</label>
                        </template>
                        <template x-if="product.flash_sale_id">
                            <label class="label label-danger !bg-danger-200 !text-white my-[8px] px-2 text-xs" x-text="$store.shop.flash_sale?.content?.use_title_badge &amp;&amp; $store.shop.flash_sale?.content?.title ? $store.shop.flash_sale.content.title : 'Flash Sale'"></label>
                        </template>
                        <template x-if="product.is_consign">
                            <label class="label label-danger !bg-highlight-50 !text-highlight-200 my-[8px] text-xs">Pre Loved</label>
                        </template>
                        <template x-if="!product.flash_sale_id &amp;&amp; !product.is_defect &amp;&amp; !product.is_consign &amp;&amp; (product.first_size?.normal_price &amp;&amp; product.first_size?.normal_price &gt; product.first_size?.price)">
                            <label class="label label-danger text-danger-200 my-[8px] text-xs">Sale</label>
                        </template>
                        <template x-if="!product.flash_sale_id &amp;&amp; !product.is_defect &amp;&amp; !product.is_consign &amp;&amp; !(product.first_size?.normal_price &amp;&amp; product.first_size?.normal_price &gt; product.first_size?.price) &amp;&amp; product.seasonal">
                            <label class="label label-success text-success-200 my-[8px] text-xs">New In</label>
                        </template>
                    </div>
                </template>
                <h3 class="mb-[8px] " x-text="product.designer_data?.name ?? '-'">Valentino</h3>
                <h4 class="mb-[16px] text-ellipsis text-nowrap truncate" x-text="product.name">Rockstud 100mm Heels
                    Patent ...</h4>

                <div class="" x-show="product.total_stock &gt; 0">
                    <p class="font-bold" :class="product.first_size?.normal_price &amp;&amp; product.first_size?.normal_price &gt; product.first_size
                            ?.price ?
                            'text-danger-200' : ''" x-text="$store.global.currency_format((product.first_size?.price ?? product.price) + (product.first_size?.markup ?? 0))">
                        IDR 11.520.000
                    </p>
                    <template x-if="product.first_size?.normal_price &amp;&amp; product.first_size?.normal_price &gt; product.first_size?.price">
                        <div class="flex text-gray-500 text-xs items-center pt-1">
                            <p class="line-through" x-text="$store.global.currency_format((product.first_size?.normal_price ?? product.price) + (product.first_size?.markup_origin ?? 0))">
                                <del>IDR 16.520.000</del>
                            </p>

                            <p class="ps-2" x-text="`(${ Math.round((1 - Number((product.first_size.price + product.first_size.markup) / (product.first_size.normal_price + product.first_size.markup_origin))) * 100 ) }% off)`">
                            </p>
                        </div>
                    </template>
                </div>
            </a>
        </div>
    </div>
</div>                        </template><div>

            let only_flash_sale = false;
            if (only_flash_sale &amp;&amp; product.product_ids_flash_sale &amp;&amp; product.data_color) {
                let flashSaleIds = product.product_ids_flash_sale.split(',').map(Number);
                product.data_color = product.data_color.filter(c =&gt; flashSaleIds.includes(Number(c.id)));
            }

            this.$watch('color_filtered', () =&gt; this.handleFilterChange());
            this.$watch('is_sale_filter_active', () =&gt; this.handleFilterChange());
            this.$watch('product_id', () =&gt; this.handleFilterChange());
            this.handleFilterChange();
        },
        handleFilterChange() {
            let found = product.data_color?.find(c =&gt; this.color_filtered.includes(c.color));
            if (!found &amp;&amp; this.is_sale_filter_active) {
                const isOnSale = c =&gt; c.first_size &amp;&amp; c.first_size.normal_price &amp;&amp; c.first_size.normal_price &gt; c.first_size.price;
                found = product.data_color?.find(c =&gt; isOnSale(c) &amp;&amp; (c.calculate_stock ?? 0) &gt; 0)
                    ?? product.data_color?.find(isOnSale);
            }
            if (found) {
                this.selectColor(found);
            } else if (product.data_color &amp;&amp; product.data_color.length &gt; 0) {
                let defaultColor = product.data_color.find(c =&gt; c.id === product.id);
                
                if (!defaultColor || (defaultColor.calculate_stock ?? 0) &lt;= 0) {
                    let inStockColor = product.data_color.find(c =&gt; (c.calculate_stock ?? 0) &gt; 0);
                    if (inStockColor) {
                        defaultColor = inStockColor;
                    }
                }

                if (defaultColor) {
                    this.selectColor(defaultColor);
                } else {
                    this.selectColor(product.data_color[0]);
                }
            }
        },
        selectColor(color) {
            if (!color) return;
            this.selected_color = color;
            product.image = color.img_url;
            product.img_url = color.img_url;
            product.first_image = color.first_image;
            product.slug = color.slug;
            product.total_stock = color.calculate_stock ?? 0;
            product.thumbnail = color.img_url;
            this.image_now = color.img_url;
            
            // Clone first_size to avoid mutating original color.first_size
            product.first_size = color.first_size ? Object.assign({}, color.first_size) : null;
            product.price = color.price;

            // Flash Sale Calculation
            // Nilai flash sale diambil dari warna yang dipilih (color.flash_sale),
            // bukan dari field level grup — tiap warna bisa punya nominal/strike berbeda.
            let fs = color.flash_sale ?? null;
            product.flash_sale_id = fs ? fs.id : null;

                let basePrice = Number(product.first_size.price ?? product.price ?? 0);
                let markup = Number(product.first_size.markup ?? 0);
                let currentTotal = basePrice + markup;
                let strikePrice = Number(fs.strike_price ?? 0);

                } else if (fs.type == 1) {
                    flashSalePrice = currentTotal * (Number(fs.value_percentage ?? 0) / 100);
                }

                if (flashSalePrice &gt; 0) {
                    let normalPrice = Number(product.first_size.normal_price ?? 0);
                    // Set normal price if it hasn't been set or if it's not a sale item yet
                    if (!normalPrice || normalPrice &lt;= basePrice) {

                        product.first_size.markup_origin = strikePrice &gt; 0 ? 0 : markup;
                    } else if (strikePrice &gt; 0) {
                        product.first_size.normal_price = strikePrice;
                        product.first_size.markup_origin = 0;

</div><div>
    <div x-data="{
        image_now: product.data_color[0]?.img_url ?? product.img_url,
        get color_filtered() {
            return $store.shop.color_selected
        },
        get is_sale_filter_active() {
            return $store.shop.filter?.condition?.includes('sale') ?? false;
        },
        get product_id() {
            return product.id;
        },
        init() {
            let only_flash_sale = false;
            if (only_flash_sale &amp;&amp; product.product_ids_flash_sale &amp;&amp; product.data_color) {
                let flashSaleIds = product.product_ids_flash_sale.split(',').map(Number);
                product.data_color = product.data_color.filter(c =&gt; flashSaleIds.includes(Number(c.id)));
            }

            this.$watch('color_filtered', () =&gt; this.handleFilterChange());
            this.$watch('is_sale_filter_active', () =&gt; this.handleFilterChange());
            this.$watch('product_id', () =&gt; this.handleFilterChange());
            this.handleFilterChange();
        },
        handleFilterChange() {
            let found = product.data_color?.find(c =&gt; this.color_filtered.includes(c.color));
            if (!found &amp;&amp; this.is_sale_filter_active) {
                const isOnSale = c =&gt; c.first_size &amp;&amp; c.first_size.normal_price &amp;&amp; c.first_size.normal_price &gt; c.first_size.price;
                found = product.data_color?.find(c =&gt; isOnSale(c) &amp;&amp; (c.calculate_stock ?? 0) &gt; 0)
                    ?? product.data_color?.find(isOnSale);
            }
            if (found) {
                this.selectColor(found);
            } else if (product.data_color &amp;&amp; product.data_color.length &gt; 0) {
                let defaultColor = product.data_color.find(c =&gt; c.id === product.id);
                
                if (!defaultColor || (defaultColor.calculate_stock ?? 0) &lt;= 0) {
                    let inStockColor = product.data_color.find(c =&gt; (c.calculate_stock ?? 0) &gt; 0);
                    if (inStockColor) {
                        defaultColor = inStockColor;
                    }
                }

                if (defaultColor) {
                    this.selectColor(defaultColor);
                } else {
                    this.selectColor(product.data_color[0]);
                }
            }
        },
        selectColor(color) {
            if (!color) return;
            this.selected_color = color;
            product.image = color.img_url;
            product.img_url = color.img_url;
            product.first_image = color.first_image;
            product.slug = color.slug;
            product.total_stock = color.calculate_stock ?? 0;
            product.thumbnail = color.img_url;
            this.image_now = color.img_url;
            
            // Clone first_size to avoid mutating original color.first_size
            product.first_size = color.first_size ? Object.assign({}, color.first_size) : null;
            product.price = color.price;

            // Flash Sale Calculation
            // Nilai flash sale diambil dari warna yang dipilih (color.flash_sale),
            // bukan dari field level grup — tiap warna bisa punya nominal/strike berbeda.
            let fs = color.flash_sale ?? null;
            product.flash_sale_id = fs ? fs.id : null;
            if (product.first_size &amp;&amp; fs) {
                let basePrice = Number(product.first_size.price ?? product.price ?? 0);
                let markup = Number(product.first_size.markup ?? 0);
                let currentTotal = basePrice + markup;
                let strikePrice = Number(fs.strike_price ?? 0);
                let flashSalePrice = 0;

                if (fs.type == 0) {
                    flashSalePrice = Number(fs.value_nominal ?? fs.value_percentage ?? 0);
                } else if (fs.type == 1) {
                    flashSalePrice = currentTotal * (Number(fs.value_percentage ?? 0) / 100);
                }

                if (flashSalePrice &gt; 0) {
                    let normalPrice = Number(product.first_size.normal_price ?? 0);
                    // Set normal price if it hasn't been set or if it's not a sale item yet
                    if (!normalPrice || normalPrice &lt;= basePrice) {
                        product.first_size.normal_price = strikePrice &gt; 0 ? strikePrice : basePrice;
                        product.first_size.markup_origin = strikePrice &gt; 0 ? 0 : markup;
                    } else if (strikePrice &gt; 0) {
                        product.first_size.normal_price = strikePrice;
                        product.first_size.markup_origin = 0;
                    }
                    let finalPrice = currentTotal - flashSalePrice;
                    product.first_size.price = finalPrice - markup;
                }
            }
        }
    }">

</div><div>
    <div x-data="{
        image_now: product.data_color[0]?.img_url ?? product.img_url,
        get color_filtered() {
            return $store.shop.color_selected
        },
        get is_sale_filter_active() {
            return $store.shop.filter?.condition?.includes('sale') ?? false;
        },
        get product_id() {
            return product.id;
        },
        init() {
            let only_flash_sale = false;
            if (only_flash_sale &amp;&amp; product.product_ids_flash_sale &amp;&amp; product.data_color) {
                let flashSaleIds = product.product_ids_flash_sale.split(',').map(Number);
                product.data_color = product.data_color.filter(c =&gt; flashSaleIds.includes(Number(c.id)));
            }

            this.$watch('color_filtered', () =&gt; this.handleFilterChange());
            this.$watch('is_sale_filter_active', () =&gt; this.handleFilterChange());
            this.$watch('product_id', () =&gt; this.handleFilterChange());
            this.handleFilterChange();
        },
        handleFilterChange() {
            let found = product.data_color?.find(c =&gt; this.color_filtered.includes(c.color));
            if (!found &amp;&amp; this.is_sale_filter_active) {
                const isOnSale = c =&gt; c.first_size &amp;&amp; c.first_size.normal_price &amp;&amp; c.first_size.normal_price &gt; c.first_size.price;
                found = product.data_color?.find(c =&gt; isOnSale(c) &amp;&amp; (c.calculate_stock ?? 0) &gt; 0)
                    ?? product.data_color?.find(isOnSale);
            }
            if (found) {
                this.selectColor(found);
            } else if (product.data_color &amp;&amp; product.data_color.length &gt; 0) {
                let defaultColor = product.data_color.find(c =&gt; c.id === product.id);
                
                if (!defaultColor || (defaultColor.calculate_stock ?? 0) &lt;= 0) {
                    let inStockColor = product.data_color.find(c =&gt; (c.calculate_stock ?? 0) &gt; 0);
                    if (inStockColor) {
                        defaultColor = inStockColor;
                    }
                }

                if (defaultColor) {
                    this.selectColor(defaultColor);
                } else {
                    this.selectColor(product.data_color[0]);
                }
            }
        },
        selectColor(color) {
            if (!color) return;
            this.selected_color = color;
            product.image = color.img_url;
            product.img_url = color.img_url;
            product.first_image = color.first_image;
            product.slug = color.slug;
            product.total_stock = color.calculate_stock ?? 0;
            product.thumbnail = color.img_url;
            this.image_now = color.img_url;
            
            // Clone first_size to avoid mutating original color.first_size
            product.first_size = color.first_size ? Object.assign({}, color.first_size) : null;
            product.price = color.price;

            // Flash Sale Calculation
            // Nilai flash sale diambil dari warna yang dipilih (color.flash_sale),
            // bukan dari field level grup — tiap warna bisa punya nominal/strike berbeda.
            let fs = color.flash_sale ?? null;
            product.flash_sale_id = fs ? fs.id : null;
            if (product.first_size &amp;&amp; fs) {
                let basePrice = Number(product.first_size.price ?? product.price ?? 0);
                let markup = Number(product.first_size.markup ?? 0);
                let currentTotal = basePrice + markup;
                let strikePrice = Number(fs.strike_price ?? 0);
                let flashSalePrice = 0;

                if (fs.type == 0) {
                    flashSalePrice = Number(fs.value_nominal ?? fs.value_percentage ?? 0);
                } else if (fs.type == 1) {
                    flashSalePrice = currentTotal * (Number(fs.value_percentage ?? 0) / 100);
                }

                if (flashSalePrice &gt; 0) {
                    let normalPrice = Number(product.first_size.normal_price ?? 0);
                    // Set normal price if it hasn't been set or if it's not a sale item yet
                    if (!normalPrice || normalPrice &lt;= basePrice) {
                        product.first_size.normal_price = strikePrice &gt; 0 ? strikePrice : basePrice;
                        product.first_size.markup_origin = strikePrice &gt; 0 ? 0 : markup;
                    } else if (strikePrice &gt; 0) {
                        product.first_size.normal_price = strikePrice;
                        product.first_size.markup_origin = 0;
                    }
                    let finalPrice = currentTotal - flashSalePrice;
                    product.first_size.price = finalPrice - markup;
                }
            }
        }
    }">

' - ' + product.item_code : '') + ' - ' + product.data_color[0]?.color" @mouseover="product.image = product.first_image?.image ?? product.img_url;" @mouseleave="product.image = product.thumbnail" :onerror="(event) =&gt; product.image = product?.thumbnail ?? product.img_url" src="https://dc3x7bfm2k0ex.cloudfront.net/products/img-1792927697-6adde7d1c2c36.webp" alt="COACH Laurel Shoulder Bag 22 With Pockets - Gold - Black" onerror="https://dc3x7bfm2k0ex.cloudfront.net/products/img-1792927697-6adde7d1c2c36.webp">

                product.data_color = product.data_color.filter(c =&gt; flashSaleIds.includes(Number(c.id)));
            }

            this.$watch('color_filtered', () =&gt; this.handleFilterChange());
            this.$watch('is_sale_filter_active', () =&gt; this.handleFilterChange());
            this.$watch('product_id', () =&gt; this.handleFilterChange());
            this.handleFilterChange();
        },
        handleFilterChange() {
            let found = product.data_color?.find(c =&gt; this.color_filtered.includes(c.color));
            if (!found &amp;&amp; this.is_sale_filter_active) {
                const isOnSale = c =&gt; c.first_size &amp;&amp; c.first_size.normal_price &amp;&amp; c.first_size.normal_price &gt; c.first_size.price;
                found = product.data_color?.find(c =&gt; isOnSale(c) &amp;&amp; (c.calculate_stock ?? 0) &gt; 0)
                    ?? product.data_color?.find(isOnSale);
            }
            if (found) {
                this.selectColor(found);
            } else if (product.data_color &amp;&amp; product.data_color.length &gt; 0) {
                let defaultColor = product.data_color.find(c =&gt; c.id === product.id);
                
                if (!defaultColor || (defaultColor.calculate_stock ?? 0) &lt;= 0) {
                    let inStockColor = product.data_color.find(c =&gt; (c.calculate_stock ?? 0) &gt; 0);
                    if (inStockColor) {
                        defaultColor = inStockColor;
                    }
                }

                if (defaultColor) {

                product.data_color = product.data_color.filter(c =&gt; flashSaleIds.includes(Number(c.id)));

            this.$watch('is_sale_filter_active', () =&gt; this.handleFilterChange());
            this.$watch('product_id', () =&gt; this.handleFilterChange());
            this.handleFilterChange();
        },
        handleFilterChange() {
            let found = product.data_color?.find(c =&gt; this.color_filtered.includes(c.color));
            if (!found &amp;&amp; this.is_sale_filter_active) {
                const isOnSale = c =&gt; c.first_size &amp;&amp; c.first_size.normal_price &amp;&amp; c.first_size.normal_price &gt; c.first_size.price;
                found = product.data_color?.find(c =&gt; isOnSale(c) &amp;&amp; (c.calculate_stock ?? 0) &gt; 0)
                    ?? product.data_color?.find(isOnSale);
            }
            if (found) {
                this.selectColor(found);
            } else if (product.data_color &amp;&amp; product.data_color.length &gt; 0) {
                let defaultColor = product.data_color.find(c =&gt; c.id === product.id);
                
                if (!defaultColor || (defaultColor.calculate_stock ?? 0) &lt;= 0) {
                    let inStockColor = product.data_color.find(c =&gt; (c.calculate_stock ?? 0) &gt; 0);
                    if (inStockColor) {
                        defaultColor = inStockColor;
                    }
                }

                if (defaultColor) {

                }

</div><div>
    <div x-data="{
        image_now: product.data_color[0]?.img_url ?? product.img_url,
        get color_filtered() {
            return $store.shop.color_selected
        },
        get is_sale_filter_active() {
            return $store.shop.filter?.condition?.includes('sale') ?? false;
        },
        get product_id() {
            return product.id;
        },
        init() {
            let only_flash_sale = false;
            if (only_flash_sale &amp;&amp; product.product_ids_flash_sale &amp;&amp; product.data_color) {
                let flashSaleIds = product.product_ids_flash_sale.split(',').map(Number);
                product.data_color = product.data_color.filter(c =&gt; flashSaleIds.includes(Number(c.id)));
            }

            this.$watch('color_filtered', () =&gt; this.handleFilterChange());
            this.$watch('is_sale_filter_active', () =&gt; this.handleFilterChange());
            this.$watch('product_id', () =&gt; this.handleFilterChange());
            this.handleFilterChange();
        },
        handleFilterChange() {
            let found = product.data_color?.find(c =&gt; this.color_filtered.includes(c.color));
            if (!found &amp;&amp; this.is_sale_filter_active) {
                const isOnSale = c =&gt; c.first_size &amp;&amp; c.first_size.normal_price &amp;&amp; c.first_size.normal_price &gt; c.first_size.price;
                found = product.data_color?.find(c =&gt; isOnSale(c) &amp;&amp; (c.calculate_stock ?? 0) &gt; 0)
                    ?? product.data_color?.find(isOnSale);
            }
            if (found) {
                this.selectColor(found);
            } else if (product.data_color &amp;&amp; product.data_color.length &gt; 0) {
                let defaultColor = product.data_color.find(c =&gt; c.id === product.id);
                
                if (!defaultColor || (defaultColor.calculate_stock ?? 0) &lt;= 0) {
                    let inStockColor = product.data_color.find(c =&gt; (c.calculate_stock ?? 0) &gt; 0);
                    if (inStockColor) {
                        defaultColor = inStockColor;
                    }
                }

                if (defaultColor) {
                    this.selectColor(defaultColor);
                } else {
                    this.selectColor(product.data_color[0]);
                }
            }
        },
        selectColor(color) {
            if (!color) return;
            this.selected_color = color;
            product.image = color.img_url;
            product.img_url = color.img_url;
            product.first_image = color.first_image;
            product.slug = color.slug;
            product.total_stock = color.calculate_stock ?? 0;
            product.thumbnail = color.img_url;
            this.image_now = color.img_url;
            
            // Clone first_size to avoid mutating original color.first_size
            product.first_size = color.first_size ? Object.assign({}, color.first_size) : null;
            product.price = color.price;

            // Flash Sale Calculation
            // Nilai flash sale diambil dari warna yang dipilih (color.flash_sale),
            // bukan dari field level grup — tiap warna bisa punya nominal/strike berbeda.
            let fs = color.flash_sale ?? null;
            product.flash_sale_id = fs ? fs.id : null;
            if (product.first_size &amp;&amp; fs) {
                let basePrice = Number(product.first_size.price ?? product.price ?? 0);
                let markup = Number(product.first_size.markup ?? 0);
                let currentTotal = basePrice + markup;
                let strikePrice = Number(fs.strike_price ?? 0);
                let flashSalePrice = 0;

                if (fs.type == 0) {
                    flashSalePrice = Number(fs.value_nominal ?? fs.value_percentage ?? 0);
                } else if (fs.type == 1) {
                    flashSalePrice = currentTotal * (Number(fs.value_percentage ?? 0) / 100);
                }

                if (flashSalePrice &gt; 0) {
                    let normalPrice = Number(product.first_size.normal_price ?? 0);
                    // Set normal price if it hasn't been set or if it's not a sale item yet
                    if (!normalPrice || normalPrice &lt;= basePrice) {
                        product.first_size.normal_price = strikePrice &gt; 0 ? strikePrice : basePrice;
                        product.first_size.markup_origin = strikePrice &gt; 0 ? 0 : markup;
                    } else if (strikePrice &gt; 0) {
                        product.first_size.normal_price = strikePrice;
                        product.first_size.markup_origin = 0;
                    }
                    let finalPrice = currentTotal - flashSalePrice;
                    product.first_size.price = finalPrice - markup;
                }
            }
        }
    }">

</div><div>
    <div x-data="{
        image_now: product.data_color[0]?.img_url ?? product.img_url,
        get color_filtered() {
            return $store.shop.color_selected
        },
        get is_sale_filter_active() {
            return $store.shop.filter?.condition?.includes('sale') ?? false;
        },
        get product_id() {
            return product.id;
        },
        init() {
            let only_flash_sale = false;
            if (only_flash_sale &amp;&amp; product.product_ids_flash_sale &amp;&amp; product.data_color) {
                let flashSaleIds = product.product_ids_flash_sale.split(',').map(Number);
                product.data_color = product.data_color.filter(c =&gt; flashSaleIds.includes(Number(c.id)));
            }

            this.$watch('color_filtered', () =&gt; this.handleFilterChange());
            this.$watch('is_sale_filter_active', () =&gt; this.handleFilterChange());
            this.$watch('product_id', () =&gt; this.handleFilterChange());
            this.handleFilterChange();
        },
        handleFilterChange() {
            let found = product.data_color?.find(c =&gt; this.color_filtered.includes(c.color));
            if (!found &amp;&amp; this.is_sale_filter_active) {
                const isOnSale = c =&gt; c.first_size &amp;&amp; c.first_size.normal_price &amp;&amp; c.first_size.normal_price &gt; c.first_size.price;
                found = product.data_color?.find(c =&gt; isOnSale(c) &amp;&amp; (c.calculate_stock ?? 0) &gt; 0)
                    ?? product.data_color?.find(isOnSale);
            }
            if (found) {
                this.selectColor(found);
            } else if (product.data_color &amp;&amp; product.data_color.length &gt; 0) {
                let defaultColor = product.data_color.find(c =&gt; c.id === product.id);
                
                if (!defaultColor || (defaultColor.calculate_stock ?? 0) &lt;= 0) {
                    let inStockColor = product.data_color.find(c =&gt; (c.calculate_stock ?? 0) &gt; 0);
                    if (inStockColor) {
                        defaultColor = inStockColor;
                    }
                }

                if (defaultColor) {
                    this.selectColor(defaultColor);
                } else {
                    this.selectColor(product.data_color[0]);
                }
            }
        },
        selectColor(color) {
            if (!color) return;
            this.selected_color = color;
            product.image = color.img_url;
            product.img_url = color.img_url;
            product.first_image = color.first_image;
            product.slug = color.slug;
            product.total_stock = color.calculate_stock ?? 0;
            product.thumbnail = color.img_url;
            this.image_now = color.img_url;
            
            // Clone first_size to avoid mutating original color.first_size
            product.first_size = color.first_size ? Object.assign({}, color.first_size) : null;
            product.price = color.price;

            // Flash Sale Calculation
            // Nilai flash sale diambil dari warna yang dipilih (color.flash_sale),
            // bukan dari field level grup — tiap warna bisa punya nominal/strike berbeda.
            let fs = color.flash_sale ?? null;
            product.flash_sale_id = fs ? fs.id : null;
            if (product.first_size &amp;&amp; fs) {
                let basePrice = Number(product.first_size.price ?? product.price ?? 0);
                let markup = Number(product.first_size.markup ?? 0);
                let currentTotal = basePrice + markup;
                let strikePrice = Number(fs.strike_price ?? 0);
                let flashSalePrice = 0;

                if (fs.type == 0) {
                    flashSalePrice = Number(fs.value_nominal ?? fs.value_percentage ?? 0);
                } else if (fs.type == 1) {
                    flashSalePrice = currentTotal * (Number(fs.value_percentage ?? 0) / 100);
                }

                if (flashSalePrice &gt; 0) {
                    let normalPrice = Number(product.first_size.normal_price ?? 0);
                    // Set normal price if it hasn't been set or if it's not a sale item yet
                    if (!normalPrice || normalPrice &lt;= basePrice) {
                        product.first_size.normal_price = strikePrice &gt; 0 ? strikePrice : basePrice;
                        product.first_size.markup_origin = strikePrice &gt; 0 ? 0 : markup;
                    } else if (strikePrice &gt; 0) {
                        product.first_size.normal_price = strikePrice;
                        product.first_size.markup_origin = 0;
                    }
                    let finalPrice = currentTotal - flashSalePrice;
                    product.first_size.price = finalPrice - markup;
                }
            }
        }
    }">

                const isOnSale = c =&gt; c.first_size &amp;&amp; c.first_size.normal_price &amp;&amp; c.first_size.normal_price &gt; c.first_size.price;
                found = product.data_color?.find(c =&gt; isOnSale(c) &amp;&amp; (c.calculate_stock ?? 0) &gt; 0)
                    ?? product.data_color?.find(isOnSale);
            }
            if (found) {
                this.selectColor(found);
            } else if (product.data_color &amp;&amp; product.data_color.length &gt; 0) {
                let defaultColor = product.data_color.find(c =&gt; c.id === product.id);
                
                if (!defaultColor || (defaultColor.calculate_stock ?? 0) &lt;= 0) {
                    let inStockColor = product.data_color.find(c =&gt; (c.calculate_stock ?? 0) &gt; 0);
                    if (inStockColor) {
                        defaultColor = inStockColor;
                    }
                }

                if (defaultColor) {
                    this.selectColor(defaultColor);
                } else {
                    this.selectColor(product.data_color[0]);
                }
            }

            if (found) {

            } else if (product.data_color &amp;&amp; product.data_color.length &gt; 0) {
                let defaultColor = product.data_color.find(c =&gt; c.id === product.id);
                
                if (!defaultColor || (defaultColor.calculate_stock ?? 0) &lt;= 0) {
                    let inStockColor = product.data_color.find(c =&gt; (c.calculate_stock ?? 0) &gt; 0);
                    if (inStockColor) {

    <div x-data="{

            return $store.shop.filter?.condition?.includes('sale') ?? false;
        },
        get product_id() {
            return product.id;
        },
        init() {
            let only_flash_sale = false;
            if (only_flash_sale &amp;&amp; product.product_ids_flash_sale &amp;&amp; product.data_color) {
                let flashSaleIds = product.product_ids_flash_sale.split(',').map(Number);
                product.data_color = product.data_color.filter(c =&gt; flashSaleIds.includes(Number(c.id)));
            }

            this.$watch('color_filtered', () =&gt; this.handleFilterChange());
            this.$watch('is_sale_filter_active', () =&gt; this.handleFilterChange());
            this.$watch('product_id', () =&gt; this.handleFilterChange());
            this.handleFilterChange();
        },
        handleFilterChange() {
            let found = product.data_color?.find(c =&gt; this.color_filtered.includes(c.color));
            if (!found &amp;&amp; this.is_sale_filter_active) {
                const isOnSale = c =&gt; c.first_size &amp;&amp; c.first_size.normal_price &amp;&amp; c.first_size.normal_price &gt; c.first_size.price;
                found = product.data_color?.find(c =&gt; isOnSale(c) &amp;&amp; (c.calculate_stock ?? 0) &gt; 0)
                    ?? product.data_color?.find(isOnSale);
            }
            if (found) {
                this.selectColor(found);
            } else if (product.data_color &amp;&amp; product.data_color.length &gt; 0) {
                let defaultColor = product.data_color.find(c =&gt; c.id === product.id);
                
                if (!defaultColor || (defaultColor.calculate_stock ?? 0) &lt;= 0) {
                    let inStockColor = product.data_color.find(c =&gt; (c.calculate_stock ?? 0) &gt; 0);
                    if (inStockColor) {
                        defaultColor = inStockColor;
                    }
                }

                if (defaultColor) {
                    this.selectColor(defaultColor);
                } else {
                    this.selectColor(product.data_color[0]);
                }
            }
        },
        selectColor(color) {
            if (!color) return;
            this.selected_color = color;
            product.image = color.img_url;
            product.img_url = color.img_url;
            product.first_image = color.first_image;
            product.slug = color.slug;
            product.total_stock = color.calculate_stock ?? 0;
            product.thumbnail = color.img_url;
            this.image_now = color.img_url;
            
            // Clone first_size to avoid mutating original color.first_size
            product.first_size = color.first_size ? Object.assign({}, color.first_size) : null;
            product.price = color.price;

            // Flash Sale Calculation
            // Nilai flash sale diambil dari warna yang dipilih (color.flash_sale),
            // bukan dari field level grup — tiap warna bisa punya nominal/strike berbeda.
            let fs = color.flash_sale ?? null;
            product.flash_sale_id = fs ? fs.id : null;
            if (product.first_size &amp;&amp; fs) {
                let basePrice = Number(product.first_size.price ?? product.price ?? 0);
                let markup = Number(product.first_size.markup ?? 0);
                let currentTotal = basePrice + markup;
                let strikePrice = Number(fs.strike_price ?? 0);
                let flashSalePrice = 0;

                    flashSalePrice = Number(fs.value_nominal ?? fs.value_percentage ?? 0);
                } else if (fs.type == 1) {
                    flashSalePrice = currentTotal * (Number(fs.value_percentage ?? 0) / 100);
                }

                if (flashSalePrice &gt; 0) {
                    let normalPrice = Number(product.first_size.normal_price ?? 0);
                    // Set normal price if it hasn't been set or if it's not a sale item yet
                    if (!normalPrice || normalPrice &lt;= basePrice) {
                        product.first_size.normal_price = strikePrice &gt; 0 ? strikePrice : basePrice;
                        product.first_size.markup_origin = strikePrice &gt; 0 ? 0 : markup;
                    } else if (strikePrice &gt; 0) {
                        product.first_size.normal_price = strikePrice;
                        product.first_size.markup_origin = 0;
                    }
                    let finalPrice = currentTotal - flashSalePrice;
                    product.first_size.price = finalPrice - markup;

</div><div>
    <div x-data="{
        image_now: product.data_color[0]?.img_url ?? product.img_url,
        get color_filtered() {
            return $store.shop.color_selected
        },
        get is_sale_filter_active() {
            return $store.shop.filter?.condition?.includes('sale') ?? false;
        },
        get product_id() {
            return product.id;
        },
        init() {
            let only_flash_sale = false;
            if (only_flash_sale &amp;&amp; product.product_ids_flash_sale &amp;&amp; product.data_color) {
                let flashSaleIds = product.product_ids_flash_sale.split(',').map(Number);
                product.data_color = product.data_color.filter(c =&gt; flashSaleIds.includes(Number(c.id)));
            }

            this.$watch('color_filtered', () =&gt; this.handleFilterChange());
            this.$watch('is_sale_filter_active', () =&gt; this.handleFilterChange());
            this.$watch('product_id', () =&gt; this.handleFilterChange());
            this.handleFilterChange();
        },
        handleFilterChange() {
            let found = product.data_color?.find(c =&gt; this.color_filtered.includes(c.color));
            if (!found &amp;&amp; this.is_sale_filter_active) {
                const isOnSale = c =&gt; c.first_size &amp;&amp; c.first_size.normal_price &amp;&amp; c.first_size.normal_price &gt; c.first_size.price;
                found = product.data_color?.find(c =&gt; isOnSale(c) &amp;&amp; (c.calculate_stock ?? 0) &gt; 0)
                    ?? product.data_color?.find(isOnSale);
            }
            if (found) {
                this.selectColor(found);
            } else if (product.data_color &amp;&amp; product.data_color.length &gt; 0) {
                let defaultColor = product.data_color.find(c =&gt; c.id === product.id);
                
                if (!defaultColor || (defaultColor.calculate_stock ?? 0) &lt;= 0) {
                    let inStockColor = product.data_color.find(c =&gt; (c.calculate_stock ?? 0) &gt; 0);
                    if (inStockColor) {
                        defaultColor = inStockColor;
                    }
                }

                if (defaultColor) {
                    this.selectColor(defaultColor);
                } else {
                    this.selectColor(product.data_color[0]);
                }
            }
        },
        selectColor(color) {
            if (!color) return;
            this.selected_color = color;
            product.image = color.img_url;
            product.img_url = color.img_url;
            product.first_image = color.first_image;
            product.slug = color.slug;
            product.total_stock = color.calculate_stock ?? 0;
            product.thumbnail = color.img_url;
            this.image_now = color.img_url;
            
            // Clone first_size to avoid mutating original color.first_size
            product.first_size = color.first_size ? Object.assign({}, color.first_size) : null;
            product.price = color.price;

            // Flash Sale Calculation
            // Nilai flash sale diambil dari warna yang dipilih (color.flash_sale),
            // bukan dari field level grup — tiap warna bisa punya nominal/strike berbeda.
            let fs = color.flash_sale ?? null;
            product.flash_sale_id = fs ? fs.id : null;
            if (product.first_size &amp;&amp; fs) {
                let basePrice = Number(product.first_size.price ?? product.price ?? 0);
                let markup = Number(product.first_size.markup ?? 0);
                let currentTotal = basePrice + markup;
                let strikePrice = Number(fs.strike_price ?? 0);
                let flashSalePrice = 0;

                if (fs.type == 0) {
                    flashSalePrice = Number(fs.value_nominal ?? fs.value_percentage ?? 0);
                } else if (fs.type == 1) {
                    flashSalePrice = currentTotal * (Number(fs.value_percentage ?? 0) / 100);
                }

                if (flashSalePrice &gt; 0) {
                    let normalPrice = Number(product.first_size.normal_price ?? 0);
                    // Set normal price if it hasn't been set or if it's not a sale item yet
                    if (!normalPrice || normalPrice &lt;= basePrice) {
                        product.first_size.normal_price = strikePrice &gt; 0 ? strikePrice : basePrice;
                        product.first_size.markup_origin = strikePrice &gt; 0 ? 0 : markup;
                    } else if (strikePrice &gt; 0) {
                        product.first_size.normal_price = strikePrice;
                        product.first_size.markup_origin = 0;
                    }
                    let finalPrice = currentTotal - flashSalePrice;
                    product.first_size.price = finalPrice - markup;
                }
            }
        }
    }">

</div><div>
    <div x-data="{
        image_now: product.data_color[0]?.img_url ?? product.img_url,
        get color_filtered() {
            return $store.shop.color_selected
        },
        get is_sale_filter_active() {
            return $store.shop.filter?.condition?.includes('sale') ?? false;
        },
        get product_id() {
            return product.id;
        },
        init() {
            let only_flash_sale = false;
            if (only_flash_sale &amp;&amp; product.product_ids_flash_sale &amp;&amp; product.data_color) {
                let flashSaleIds = product.product_ids_flash_sale.split(',').map(Number);
                product.data_color = product.data_color.filter(c =&gt; flashSaleIds.includes(Number(c.id)));
            }

            this.$watch('color_filtered', () =&gt; this.handleFilterChange());
            this.$watch('is_sale_filter_active', () =&gt; this.handleFilterChange());
            this.$watch('product_id', () =&gt; this.handleFilterChange());
            this.handleFilterChange();
        },
        handleFilterChange() {
            let found = product.data_color?.find(c =&gt; this.color_filtered.includes(c.color));
            if (!found &amp;&amp; this.is_sale_filter_active) {
                const isOnSale = c =&gt; c.first_size &amp;&amp; c.first_size.normal_price &amp;&amp; c.first_size.normal_price &gt; c.first_size.price;
                found = product.data_color?.find(c =&gt; isOnSale(c) &amp;&amp; (c.calculate_stock ?? 0) &gt; 0)
                    ?? product.data_color?.find(isOnSale);
            }
            if (found) {
                this.selectColor(found);
            } else if (product.data_color &amp;&amp; product.data_color.length &gt; 0) {
                let defaultColor = product.data_color.find(c =&gt; c.id === product.id);
                
                if (!defaultColor || (defaultColor.calculate_stock ?? 0) &lt;= 0) {
                    let inStockColor = product.data_color.find(c =&gt; (c.calculate_stock ?? 0) &gt; 0);
                    if (inStockColor) {
                        defaultColor = inStockColor;
                    }
                }

                if (defaultColor) {
                    this.selectColor(defaultColor);
                } else {
                    this.selectColor(product.data_color[0]);
                }
            }
        },
        selectColor(color) {
            if (!color) return;
            this.selected_color = color;
            product.image = color.img_url;
            product.img_url = color.img_url;
            product.first_image = color.first_image;
            product.slug = color.slug;
            product.total_stock = color.calculate_stock ?? 0;
            product.thumbnail = color.img_url;
            this.image_now = color.img_url;
            
            // Clone first_size to avoid mutating original color.first_size
            product.first_size = color.first_size ? Object.assign({}, color.first_size) : null;
            product.price = color.price;

            // Flash Sale Calculation
            // Nilai flash sale diambil dari warna yang dipilih (color.flash_sale),
            // bukan dari field level grup — tiap warna bisa punya nominal/strike berbeda.
            let fs = color.flash_sale ?? null;
            product.flash_sale_id = fs ? fs.id : null;
            if (product.first_size &amp;&amp; fs) {
                let basePrice = Number(product.first_size.price ?? product.price ?? 0);
                let markup = Number(product.first_size.markup ?? 0);
                let currentTotal = basePrice + markup;
                let strikePrice = Number(fs.strike_price ?? 0);
                let flashSalePrice = 0;

                if (fs.type == 0) {
                    flashSalePrice = Number(fs.value_nominal ?? fs.value_percentage ?? 0);
                } else if (fs.type == 1) {
                    flashSalePrice = currentTotal * (Number(fs.value_percentage ?? 0) / 100);
                }

                if (flashSalePrice &gt; 0) {
                    let normalPrice = Number(product.first_size.normal_price ?? 0);
                    // Set normal price if it hasn't been set or if it's not a sale item yet
                    if (!normalPrice || normalPrice &lt;= basePrice) {
                        product.first_size.normal_price = strikePrice &gt; 0 ? strikePrice : basePrice;
                        product.first_size.markup_origin = strikePrice &gt; 0 ? 0 : markup;
                    } else if (strikePrice &gt; 0) {
                        product.first_size.normal_price = strikePrice;
                        product.first_size.markup_origin = 0;
                    }
                    let finalPrice = currentTotal - flashSalePrice;
                    product.first_size.price = finalPrice - markup;
                }
            }
        }
    }">

</div><div>
    <div x-data="{
        image_now: product.data_color[0]?.img_url ?? product.img_url,
        get color_filtered() {
            return $store.shop.color_selected
        },
        get is_sale_filter_active() {
            return $store.shop.filter?.condition?.includes('sale') ?? false;
        },
        get product_id() {
            return product.id;
        },
        init() {
            let only_flash_sale = false;
            if (only_flash_sale &amp;&amp; product.product_ids_flash_sale &amp;&amp; product.data_color) {
                let flashSaleIds = product.product_ids_flash_sale.split(',').map(Number);
                product.data_color = product.data_color.filter(c =&gt; flashSaleIds.includes(Number(c.id)));
            }

            this.$watch('color_filtered', () =&gt; this.handleFilterChange());
            this.$watch('is_sale_filter_active', () =&gt; this.handleFilterChange());
            this.$watch('product_id', () =&gt; this.handleFilterChange());
            this.handleFilterChange();
        },
        handleFilterChange() {
            let found = product.data_color?.find(c =&gt; this.color_filtered.includes(c.color));
            if (!found &amp;&amp; this.is_sale_filter_active) {
                const isOnSale = c =&gt; c.first_size &amp;&amp; c.first_size.normal_price &amp;&amp; c.first_size.normal_price &gt; c.first_size.price;
                found = product.data_color?.find(c =&gt; isOnSale(c) &amp;&amp; (c.calculate_stock ?? 0) &gt; 0)
                    ?? product.data_color?.find(isOnSale);
            }
            if (found) {
                this.selectColor(found);
            } else if (product.data_color &amp;&amp; product.data_color.length &gt; 0) {
                let defaultColor = product.data_color.find(c =&gt; c.id === product.id);
                
                if (!defaultColor || (defaultColor.calculate_stock ?? 0) &lt;= 0) {
                    let inStockColor = product.data_color.find(c =&gt; (c.calculate_stock ?? 0) &gt; 0);
                    if (inStockColor) {
                        defaultColor = inStockColor;
                    }
                }

                if (defaultColor) {
                    this.selectColor(defaultColor);
                } else {
                    this.selectColor(product.data_color[0]);
                }
            }
        },
        selectColor(color) {
            if (!color) return;
            this.selected_color = color;
            product.image = color.img_url;
            product.img_url = color.img_url;
            product.first_image = color.first_image;
            product.slug = color.slug;
            product.total_stock = color.calculate_stock ?? 0;
            product.thumbnail = color.img_url;
            this.image_now = color.img_url;
            
            // Clone first_size to avoid mutating original color.first_size
            product.first_size = color.first_size ? Object.assign({}, color.first_size) : null;
            product.price = color.price;

            // Flash Sale Calculation
            // Nilai flash sale diambil dari warna yang dipilih (color.flash_sale),
            // bukan dari field level grup — tiap warna bisa punya nominal/strike berbeda.
            let fs = color.flash_sale ?? null;
            product.flash_sale_id = fs ? fs.id : null;
            if (product.first_size &amp;&amp; fs) {
                let basePrice = Number(product.first_size.price ?? product.price ?? 0);
                let markup = Number(product.first_size.markup ?? 0);
                let currentTotal = basePrice + markup;
                let strikePrice = Number(fs.strike_price ?? 0);
                let flashSalePrice = 0;

                if (fs.type == 0) {
                    flashSalePrice = Number(fs.value_nominal ?? fs.value_percentage ?? 0);
                } else if (fs.type == 1) {
                    flashSalePrice = currentTotal * (Number(fs.value_percentage ?? 0) / 100);
                }

                if (flashSalePrice &gt; 0) {
                    let normalPrice = Number(product.first_size.normal_price ?? 0);
                    // Set normal price if it hasn't been set or if it's not a sale item yet
                    if (!normalPrice || normalPrice &lt;= basePrice) {
                        product.first_size.normal_price = strikePrice &gt; 0 ? strikePrice : basePrice;
                        product.first_size.markup_origin = strikePrice &gt; 0 ? 0 : markup;
                    } else if (strikePrice &gt; 0) {
                        product.first_size.normal_price = strikePrice;
                        product.first_size.markup_origin = 0;
                    }
                    let finalPrice = currentTotal - flashSalePrice;
                    product.first_size.price = finalPrice - markup;
                }
            }
        }
    }">
